<intent-contract>

## Intent

**Problem:** Story 14.5 owns 225 SupportCollectionTest Test IDs and XT-collections-9..11. Transformation, aggregation and callback members remain unported, although earlier Stories already delivered grouping, keying, counting, percentage and sortBy behavior.

**Approach:** Translate every independent test in Upstream order and implement the Collection, LazyCollection, Enumerable and shared trait members it reaches. Raise a contract Escalation for missing PHP builtins before touching affected Test IDs; hold their members whole and continue independent work. The Story remains blocked until those contracts land, preserving its single spec for resumption.

## Boundaries & Constraints

**Always:** Limit edits to packages/collections/**, Story 14.5's Expectations, conformance/oracle-requests/14.5.jsonl, its own Escalations, and orchestrator bookkeeping. Keep Upstream assertions, values, ordering, public names and exception messages. Use runtime PHP helpers and value copies, null for absence, SyncCallback/NotPromise for sync callbacks, options forms where AD-26 requires them, and sequential async companions. Apply existing DIV clauses precisely. Preserve the completed 14.1–14.4 methods and tests. Read AD-6, AD-9, AD-22 and AD-26 in the spine before writing package code.

**Never:** Edit another Component or Protected grader data, implement a missing PHP builtin inside collections, stub another Component, weaken an assertion, or invent a divergence. A missing runtime contract stays not-ported-yet with escalation metadata; runtime is an earlier Epic, so blocked-by:runtime is invalid. A later Component dependency is blocked-by:<component>, with the Upstream body retained and a throwing BlockedByComponentError seam.

</intent-contract>

## Code Map

- `stories/14.5.md` -- canonical 225 Test IDs and XT-collections-9..11; read-only except status. `conformance/upstream/framework/tests/Support/SupportCollectionTest.php` -- exact complete bodies and fixtures, including locale comparisons, enums and reduceSpread; never derive bodies from names.
- `conformance/upstream/framework/src/Illuminate/Collections/{Collection,LazyCollection,Enumerable}.php` and `Traits/EnumeratesValues.php` -- authoritative algorithms, eager/lazy boundaries, scalar coercion, ordering and signatures.
- `packages/collections/src/{Collection,LazyCollection,Enumerable}.ts`, `Traits/EnumeratesValues.ts`, `Arr.ts` -- existing independent implementation retained; complete held members and public declarations. `LazyCollection.makeIterator` currently wraps callable-produced JS generators with `new Generator`; use `Generator.owned` for those temporary sources, preserving explicit user-held runtime Generators.
- `packages/collections/src/support/callables.ts` -- replace outdated private function/object-only invocation with runtime callable resolution; retain NotPromise/type-aware lint callback protections and copies.
- `packages/runtime/src/php/{index,arrayRefFunctions,arrayFunctions,callables,arithmetic,Generator}.ts` -- READ ONLY: item 10 delivered runtime builtins, arithmetic, append/unpack, callable resolution and owned-generator lifetime. Read actual exports/signatures, reuse rather than copying PHP semantics. `resolveCallable`, `callUnpacked`, `arrayAppend`, `plus`, `divide`, Ref sorts and Generator.owned are now available.
- `packages/collections/tests/ported/framework/tests/Support/SupportCollectionTest.test.ts` -- existing passing bodies and held placeholders; replace held placeholders with exact upstream translations in original assertion order. Existing throwing Support setup is the seam for genuine class_basename-dependent tests.
- `packages/collections/tests/xaravel/collections.test.ts` -- XT9 signed sorting fixture now exists; retain assertions. Add XT10 enum mapInto and extend XT11 lifetime coverage (owned finally on early exit; user-held resumability; DIV-9.16 exception precedence).
- `conformance/expectations/collections/framework/tests/Support/SupportCollectionTest.jsonl` -- own lines only; no not-ported-yet after completion. `packages/collections/docs/collections.md` and `async-surface.json` -- keep documentation/declarations aligned.

## Tasks & Acceptance

**Execution:**
- `packages/collections/tests/ported/framework/tests/Support/SupportCollectionTest.test.ts` -- translate all 114 held lines' complete Upstream tests using delivered runtime APIs, including callable-array fixtures, strrev/strtolower, locale calls, sorting, chunking, sliding, splitting, mapping, aggregates and reducers. Preserve every existing assertion. Run fast Story baseline after translation and before implementing.
- `packages/collections/src/{Collection,LazyCollection,Enumerable}.ts`, `Traits/EnumeratesValues.ts`, `Arr.ts` -- complete median, mapToDictionary/mapToGroups, mapInto (including backed enums), sliding, split/splitIn, chunk/chunkWhile/chunkBy, sortKeys families, spread methods, sum/avg/average and reduceSpread in Upstream operation order. Reuse delivered runtime builtins/arithmetic/unpack helpers for every dependency. Keep all independent prior methods/tests and review fixes. Keep callbacks synchronous with NotPromise overloads and AD-26 options where required.
- `packages/collections/src/support/callables.ts`, `LazyCollection.ts` -- use shared runtime callable resolution wherever collections invokes PHP callables; use runtime ownership only for temporary JS generator sources created by framework enumeration, preserving user-held Generator resumability. Add exact previously held ESC-14.5-4 finally regression.
- `packages/collections/tests/ported/framework/tests/Support/SupportCollectionTest.test.ts`, own Expectations -- Only the invalid-result reduceSpread test uses the throwing Support seam; the successful test never reaches class_basename and must execute normally with pass Expectations. A newly discovered missing contract is escalated, never locally implemented. Existing ESC-14.5-1..4 are resolved historical records, not reasons to leave completed APIs unported.
- `packages/collections/tests/xaravel/collections.test.ts` -- register XT10 with int/string-backed enum constructors/from/tryFrom behavior matching Upstream and AD-26. Retain signed XT9 comparisons; complete XT11 async sequential/copy/error/lifetime assertions and callback compile/lint probes for new sync APIs.
- Own Expectations and `packages/collections/docs/collections.md` -- record only actual passing or valid later-component-blocked tests, remove stale runtime-held documentation, preserve prior assertions/adaptations and historical review verdicts.

**Acceptance Criteria:**
- Given all 225 manifest Test IDs, when the full Story harness runs, then every ID matches Expectations with no not-ported-yet reason and only real later-component blockers.
- Given numeric/natural/multiple-key sorting and signed XT9 fixture, when both classes run, then values, keys, shape and stable ties match Oracle.
- Given int/string-backed enum classes, when XT10 maps into them, then static enum cases/from/tryFrom and identity match Upstream.
- Given async map/each over keyed input, when XT11 runs, then callbacks are sequential, copied results preserve keys, source/callback errors propagate, and early stop runs temporary-generator finally while user-held Generators remain resumable, subject to DIV-9.16.
- Given final code and all retained/new assertions, when typecheck, lint, full Story harness, full harness, story:check and fidelity status run, then verification passes and current counted Fidelity approvals exist without weakening the grader.

## Review repair requirements

- `LazyCollection.ts` -- re-derive `sliding`, `chunk`, `chunkWhile` so early stop and callback errors close nested owned temporaries through public runtime iterator/entries mechanisms, without importing internal runtime files, exhausting sources, closing user-held generators or changing Upstream advancement boundaries. If the public runtime contracts cannot implement that faithfully, raise a contract Escalation instead of inventing a close protocol.
- `support/callables.ts`, affected Collection/LazyCollection/Enumerable/trait declarations -- supported PHP callable strings, arrays and invokable objects must typecheck at the public methods this Story implements or changes (`map`, `filter`, dictionary/groups, spread, chunkWhile, aggregate callbacks and key comparators). Preserve contextual function types and NotPromise/lint rejection. Reuse a shared callback type for non-function forms; no second runtime resolver. `avg`/`average` callbacks take value only, matching Upstream. Successful `reduceSpread` declares its supported PHP-array return type rather than unknown.
- `tests/xaravel/collections.test.ts` -- add direct successful reduceSpread assertions for both classes while running the successful Upstream reduceSpread test normally and retaining the Support blocker only for its invalid-result test. Add composed chunk/sliding/chunkWhile early-stop and callback-error/finally coverage, user-held resumability, successful-false cleanup-error propagation, dictionary/groups reused mutable callback-result copies, string-enum mapped keys, and compile/lint probes for the public callable forms/value-only avg signature. No assertion is removed. Wrong backing-type behavior of existing test enum fixtures is pre-existing; no invented strict-mode requirement.
- Preserve XT9's signed fixture assertions. Its unchanged request/body/output missing from the Fidelity prompt is a permitted nonblocking F-7 evidence gap; carry the note to INBOX, no tool change or waiver in this Story.

## Design Notes

Hold both class implementations of a dependent member, even when the lazy side could be built alone, following Story 14.4's whole-member rule. Tests that only reach an early return remain held with their member; this avoids presenting a partial implementation as complete. Independent map/each and other families still progress. class_basename belongs to Support (Epic 20); reduceSpread's invalid-result test also needs it once the runtime callback builtin arrives.
